● Server is up. Here's what the E-LAB gives you:

  Left panel — Skill Catalog
  - All skills listed with domain colour-coding (Clinical, Genomics, Drug
  Discovery, EHR/PMR, Compliance, NLP, Lab, General)
  - Search by name/title/description
  - Domain filter chips
  - Recent run history at the bottom (last 5 runs with success/fail indicators)

  Right panel — Lab Workspace
  - Skill header — name, title, description, capability tags pulled from
  SKILL.md headings, plus live stats (runs / avg latency / success rate)
  - Prompt input — free-text with a skill-specific placeholder hint (e.g. for
  Genomics: "Analyse BRCA1 c.5266dupC variant…"), ⌘↵ to run
  - Document upload — drag & drop or browse; accepts PDF, FASTA, VCF, CSV,
  DICOM, images (up to 5 files × 50 MB each); files are passed to the skill's
  workspace
  - Compliance panel — three toggles:
    - Audit Trail — logs the run (input hash, skill, user) to the signed audit
  ledger
    - PII Masking — instructs the skill to redact identifiable information from
  output
    - Strict Compliance — requires the skill to flag ICH E6/HIPAA/GDPR risks
  before proceeding
  - Run/Stop button — real execution via runAgent() in Docker, not mocked
  - Live output panel — SSE streaming so output appears as the skill runs; shows
  uploaded file confirmations, error details, downloadable artifacts, and run
  time

● Server is up. Here's what was just added — the features that make this a real 
  medical AI platform, not just another SaaS:

  ---
  Patient Intelligence Hub (/patients)

  - AMGI UID format patient registry (AMGI-GB-2024-0001234)
  - Risk stratification — 0–100 score bar with CRITICAL / HIGH / MEDIUM / LOW
  badge, risk factor tags
  - 5 one-click AI analysis tools per patient — each runs a real skill:
    - Risk Score — genomic + clinical risk across 5 domains
    - Trial Match — eligibility against active phase II/III trials
    - Drug Safety — drug-drug and pharmacogenomic interaction audit
    - Care Plan — AI-generated 0–7d / 1–3m / 3–12m treatment roadmap
    - 2nd Opinion — independent AI clinical review
  - Analysis history — every run stored and timestamped
  - Conditions, medications, genomic profile all stored per patient

  ---
  Autonomous Pipelines (/pipelines)

  5 pre-built clinical pipeline templates — each chains 4 real skills
  autonomously:
  1. Genomic Risk Report — Variant analysis → clinical significance →
  pharmacogenomics → formal report
  2. Patient Admission Workflow — EHR registration → risk assessment →
  differential diagnosis → compliance check
  3. Drug Discovery Screen — Target analysis → protein structure → compound
  screen → ADMET prediction
  4. Clinical Trial Matching — Profile extraction → eligibility → regulatory
  review → patient brief
  5. Regulatory Compliance Audit — Document review → security audit → gap
  analysis → remediation roadmap

  Each step runs sequentially, passing its output automatically as input to the 
  next step. Live streaming shows each node completing in real-time.

  ---
  E-LAB (/elab) (built in previous session)

  — Now visible in nav, with compliance toggles, file upload, and direct real
  skill execution.
